    F = np.fft.rfft(a, fftlen)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, fftlen)[:2*n-1]
    return b
```

```
def grad_from_weights(a, p, Fx=None, Lgrad=grad_fftlen):
    # p length Lc=2n-1
    rp = np.zeros(Lgrad, dtype=float)
    rp[:Lc] = p[::-1]
    if Fx is None: Fx = rfft(a, Lgrad)
    Fp = rfft(rp, Lgrad)
    conv = irfft(Fx * Fp, Lgrad)
    # conv index s = Lc-1 - i? parent: C = conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
    return 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```

Pseudo:
```
def refine(a, deadline, min_time=...):
    a = normalized copy
    best = a.copy(); best_score = fft_score(a)
    L = fft_len; Lc=2n-1; precompute? 
    def conv_score(x):
       F=rfft(x,L); b=irfft(F*F,L)[:Lc]; return b.max()
    def compute_G(x, p, F=None):
       if F is None: F=rfft(x,L)
       rp=np.zeros(L); rp[:Lc]=p[::-1]
       Fp=rfft(rp,L)
       G=2*irfft(F*Fp,L)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       return G, F
    tau_frac = 0.1
    alpha = 0.5
    stagnant=0
    while time < deadline:
        F=rfft(a,L); b=irfft(F*F,L)[:Lc]; M=b.max()
        score=2n*M
        if score < best_score - ...: update best
        # weights p: softmax(M? b)
        # Use top active selection perhaps faster/more exact:
        # p = b.copy(); p[p < M - tol]=0; p/=sum? But if only one lag, update may stagnate.
        # use softmax with tau = max(tau_frac*M, 1e-12*n?)
        tau=tau_frac*M
        z=(b-M)/tau
        p=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,0)); p/=p.sum()
        G,_=compute_G(a,p,F)
        # normalize G to mean? G_sum =1 (since sum_i G_i = sum p_k c_k = E_p ≤M? Wait sum_i G_i =Σ_k p_k Σ_i a_{k-i} =Σ p_k sum valid a over indices? For each k, Σ_i a_{k-i} = S=1, so sum_i G_i=1. Good.
        meanG=1/n
        # candidate log update:
        # to avoid huge if G ~0, eps = 1e-12*meanG
        Gs=G+eps
        ratio=meanG/Gs # high small reduce
        # loga can compute each iter maybe expensive: np.log okay.
        cand = a * np.exp(alpha*(np.log(ratio))) # = a * ratio^alpha
        cand /= cand.sum()
        # maybe also try linear projected? We'll line search alphas.
        alphas = [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*2]
        best_local_m = M
        for aa in alphas:
            c = normalize(a * (meanG/(G+eps))**aa)
            m=fft_max(c) # requires new FFT; 3 per iter expensive but okay if reduce
            if m < best_local_m - 1e-15:
                a=c; best_local_m=m; break? maybe choose smallest alpha? 
        # if no improvement, halve tau? alpha? 
```

This is a promising simple algorithm:
```
for iter:
  compute c
  p = c ** q
  p /= sum
  G = corr(p,a)
  update a = normalize(a * (meanG / (G+eps)) ** alpha)
  line search exact max or F_w
```
For q=1, this minimizes sum c_k^2. For q large, max.

Loop structure efficient:
```

while time:
   # compute weights p from b
   if mode l2: G=G_fast(FA)

Let's sketch code fully:

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _next_pow2(x):
    p=1
    while p<x: p <<=1
    return p

def _as_nonneg_normalized(arr, fallback_n=64):
    try:
       a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
       if a.ndim!=1: a=a.ravel()
       if a.size==0: a=np.ones(fallback_n)
       a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s < 1e-8:
           a=np.ones(a.size if a.size>0 else fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
           s=a.sum()
       a = a / s
    except Exception:
       a=np.ones(fallback_n,dtype=np.float64)/fallback_n
    # ensure values finite
    return a

def _internal_score(a):
    n=a.size
    L=_next_pow2(max(2*n-1,3*n-2))
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    M=b.max()
    return 2.0*n*M, M, b, F, L
```
But local_search needs L many times, not recompute. We'll set once.

Local_search:
```
def _local_search(a0, until, rng, alpha_init=1.0):
    a = _normalize(a0.copy())
    n = a.size
    if n<=0: return None
    L = _next_pow2(max(2*n-1, 3*n-2))
    Lc = 2*n-1
    FA = np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(FA*FA,L)[:Lc]
    b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
    M = float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = 2.0*n*M
    alpha = alpha_init
    reject_stag=0
    max_stag=0
    perturb_sigma=0.03
    perturb_count=0
    iter_count=0
    # maybe add tiny to all at start to avoid zeros
    a = a + 1e-12/n
    a = _normalize_inplace(a)
    FA = rfft(a)... recompute? if tiny negligible, no. But if many zeros, better recompute.
```

Functions inside to capture n,L,Lc:
```
def eval_state(x, FA=None):
   if FA is None: FA=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
   b=np.fft.irfft(FA*FA,L)[:Lc]
   b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
   M=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
   return b,M,FA

def grad_l2(FA):
   G=np.fft.irfft((FA*FA)*np.conj(FA), L)[:n]
   return np.maximum(G,0.0)
```
Wait `(FA*FA)*np.conj(FA)` may overflow? |FA|^4 scale: For a sum1, FA values ≤1; product magnitude ≤1, no.

Generic:
```
def grad_p(p):

```

def weights_power(b, q):
   if q == 1.0:
      p = b
   else:
      bpos = b + 1e-300
      lb = np.log(bpos)
      lp = q * lb
      lp -= lp.max()
      p = np.exp(lp)
      s=p.sum()
      if not finite or s<=0: p=softmax_fallback; else p/=s
   return p
```

Softmax weights:
```
def weights_softmax(b, M, tau_frac):
   tau = max(tau_frac * M, 1e-30)
   z = (b - M)/tau
   z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
   p = np.exp(z)
   s = p.sum()
   if s<=0: p = np.zeros(Lc); p[argmax(b)] =1 # but softmax max exp z=1
   else p/=s
```

Make candidate:
```
def make_candidate(a, G, alpha, style):
    gm = G.mean()
    if not finite or gm <= 0: return a.copy(), 0.0
    x = G / gm
    if style == 0:
       factor = 1.0 + alpha * (1.0 - x)
       # factor can be negative; clip lower small, upper maybe 1+alpha? If x can 0, factor=1+alpha.
       max_factor = max(1.0, 1.0 + alpha) * 2.0? But x might 0 -> factor 1+alpha. If alpha large 2 ->3. Could be fine.
       np.clip(factor, 1e-8, 100.0, out=factor)
    else:
       # center log
       logx = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-12))
       center = logx.mean()
       logfactor = -alpha * (logx - center)
       logfactor = np.clip(logfactor, -10.0, 10.0)
       factor = np.exp(logfactor)
    cand = a * factor
    s = cand.sum()
    if not finite or s < 1e-12:
       return a.copy(), 0.1
    cand /= s
    # optional floor: cand = np.maximum(cand, 0)
    return cand, 1.0
```
For style 0, if alpha>1, x>1+1/alpha gives negative, clipped to 0. This may zero many. Maybe use alpha≤1 for linear; if alpha grows >1, switch to log. We can cap alpha: max_alpha=1.5 for style0, 2.0 for style1? Use global min/max. Simpler cap alpha to [0.01, 2.0], style0 if alpha>1: use style1. Or in candidate: if style=0 and alpha>1 use log. Let's set `style = 1 if (style==0 and alpha>1.0) else style`. Alpha max 2.0.

Adaptive alpha:
- On accept: `alpha = min(alpha*1.1, 2.0)`, maybe if M improved a lot `*1.2`.

while time.monotonic() < until:
    iter_count +=1
    # compute weights and G
    if max_stag < 30:
       # L2 fast
       G = grad_l2(FA); p = b; sec_old = b.dot(b)
       style = 0 if alpha<=1 else 1
    elif max_stag < 120:

    elif max_stag < 300:

    elif max_stag < 800:

    else:
       p = weights_softmax(b,M, max(0.01, tau_frac*0.7)); G=grad_p(p,FA); sec_old=dot(p,b); style=1
    # sec_old maybe if p=b and b sum not1; okay
    # candidate
    cand = make_candidate(a,G,alpha,style)
    FA_new = rfft(cand,L)
    b_new = irfft(FA_new*FA_new,L)[:Lc]; clip
    M_new = b_new.max()
    sec_new = dot(p, b_new)
    if M_new < M - tolM:
       accept? also update best
       improved_max=True
    elif M_new <= M + tolM and sec_new < sec_old - tolSec:
       accept, improved_max=False
    else reject
    if accept:
       a=cand; FA=FA_new; b=b_new; M=M_new; sec? b?
       reject_stag=0

          score =2*n*M

          max_stag=0

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p=1
    while p < x: p <<=1
    return p

def _normalize(a, fallback_n=64):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)  # not needed
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size if a.size>0 else fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s
    return a
```
Need import math or use np.isfinite. Use math.

`_local_search`:
```
def _local_search(a0, until, rng, alpha_init=1.0, max_alpha=2.0):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0: return None
    L = _next_pow2(max(2*n-1, 3*n-2))
    Lc = 2*n-1
    # add floor to support
    a = a + 1e-12/n
    a = a / a.sum()
    FA = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b_full = np.fft.irfft(FA*FA, L)
    b_full = np.maximum(b_full, 0.0, out=b_full)
    b = b_full[:Lc]
    M = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    best_score = 2.0*n*M
    best_a = a.copy()
    alpha = float(alpha_init)
    tau_frac = 0.25
    reject_stag = 0
    max_stag = 0
    perturb_sigma = 0.03
    perturb_count = 0
    rp_buf = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    tolM = 1e-15
    tolSec = 1e-18

    def state_from(x):
       FAx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
       bf = np.fft.irfft(FAx*FAx, L)
       bf = np.maximum(bf, 0.0, out=bf)
       bb = bf[:Lc]
       Mx = float(bb.max()) if bb.size else 0.0
       return FAx, bb, Mx

    def grad_l2(FAx):
       Gf = np.fft.irfft((FAx*FAx)*np.conj(FAx), L)
       G = Gf[:n]
       G = np.maximum(G, 0.0, out=G)
       return G

    def grad_p(p):
       if Lc <= L:
          rp_buf[:Lc] = p[::-1]
          rp_buf[Lc:] = 0.0
       else: ... # L enough
       Fp = np.fft.rfft(rp_buf, L)
       Gf = np.fft.irfft(FA * Fp, L)  # Uses outer FA? Need nonlocal FA current; but function called with current FA. Better pass FA.
       G = Gf[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       G = np.maximum(G, 0.0, out=G)
       return G
```
But `grad_p` uses current `FA`; if FA variable reassigned, closure sees current? In Python, closure reads nonlocal if defined in scope; if `FA` assigned in outer and not modified inside function, it reads current value. Since `FA` is assigned in outer loop (`FA = ...`), and grad_p does not assign, it will use the latest value from enclosing scope. However if `FA` is local variable in `_local_search`, closure captures by reference. Good. But to be safe, pass `FAx`.

    def weights_power(bvec, q):
       if q <= 1.0000001:
          p = bvec
       else:
          bpos = bvec + 1e-300
          lb = np.log(bpos)
          lp = q * lb
          lp -= lp.max()
          p = np.exp(lp)
          s = p.sum()
          if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
             p = np.zeros(Lc); idx = np.argmax(bvec); p[idx]=1.0
          else: p = p / s
       return p
```

    def weights_softmax(bvec, Mx, tfrac):
       tau = max(tfrac * Mx, 1e-30)
       z = (bvec - Mx) / tau
       z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
       p = np.exp(z)
       s = p.sum()
       if not finite or s <=0:
          p = np.zeros(Lc); p[np.argmax(bvec)] = 1.0
       else: p = p / s
       return p

Candidate maker:
```
    def make_candidate(a_cur, Gv, al, style):
       gm = float(Gv.mean())
       if not math.isfinite(gm) or gm <= 1e-300:
           return a_cur.copy(), False
       x = Gv / gm
       if style == 0:
           if al > 1.0:
               # use log for larger alpha
               logx = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-12))
               center = float(logx.mean())
               lf = -al*(logx-center)
               lf = np.clip(lf, -10.0, 10.0)
               factor = np.exp(lf)
           else:
               factor = 1.0 + al*(1.0 - x)
               factor = np.clip(factor, 1e-8, 100.0, out=factor)
       else:
           logx = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-12))
           center = float(logx.mean())
           lf = -al*(logx-center)
           lf = np.clip(lf, -10.0, 10.0)
           factor = np.exp(lf)
       cand = a_cur * factor
       s = float(cand.sum())
       if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
           return a_cur.copy(), False
       cand /= s
       return cand, True
```
Potential if `factor` has NaN due x, we handle.

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < until:
       it +=1
       # determine mode
       if max_stag < 30:
           q=1.0; use_generic=False; style=0; p=b; sec_old=float(np.dot(p,b))
           G=grad_l2(FA)
       elif max_stag < 100:
           q=2.0; p=weights_power(b,q); sec_old=dot; G=grad_p(p, FA); style=0 if alpha<=1 else 1
       elif max_stag < 250:
           q=5.0; p=weights_power(b,q); sec_old=dot; G=grad_p(p,FA); style=1
       elif max_stag < 700:
           p=weights_softmax(b,M,tau_frac); sec_old=dot; G=grad_p(p,FA); style=1
       else:
           p=weights_softmax(b,M,max(0.02,tau_frac*0.7)); sec_old=dot; G=grad_p(p,FA); style=1
       # candidate
       cand, ok = make_candidate(a,G,alpha,style)
       if not ok:
           alpha = max(alpha*0.5, 0.02); reject_stag +=1; max_stag +=1
           if reject_stag>30: perturb(); continue
           continue
       FA_new, b_new, M_new = state_from(cand)
       sec_new = float(np.dot(p, b_new))
       improved_max = M_new < M - tolM
       if improved_max:
           accept=True
       else:
           if M_new <= M + tolM and sec_new < sec_old - tolSec:
               accept=True
           elif M_new <= M + tolM and sec_new < sec_old and (M_new < M or True?): # maybe no tol; use >? 
               accept=False
           else: accept=False
       if accept:
           a,FA,b,M = cand,FA_new,b_new,M_new
           reject_stag=0
           if improved_max:
               score=2.0*n*M
               if score < best_score - 1e-15:
                   best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
               max_stag=0
               alpha = min(max_alpha, alpha*1.15)
               tau_frac = max(0.02, tau_frac*0.97)
           else:
               max_stag += 1
               alpha = min(max_alpha*0.8, alpha*1.02) # maybe don't increase much
       else:
           reject_stag +=1
           max_stag +=1
           alpha = max(0.02, alpha*0.6)
           if reject_stag > 120:
               # try more aggressive style/power? tau
               tau_frac = min(0.8, tau_frac*1.7)
               if reject_stag > 250 or alpha < 0.05:
                   perturb()
           elif reject_stag > 60:
               # switch style for next
               style = 1 if style==0 else 0
       # periodic sym candidate
       if it % 150 == 0 and (max_stag > 25 or reject_stag > 10):
           cand_s = 0.5*(a + a[::-1])
           cand_s = _normalize(cand_s)
           FA_s,b_s,M_s = state_from(cand_s)
           # accept if M_s much smaller or L2 smaller and M not larger
           if M_s < M - tolM:
               a,FA,b,M = cand_s,FA_s,b_s,M_s
               reject_stag=0; max_stag=0
               score=2*n*M...
           elif M_s <= M + tolM and (b_s.dot(b_s) < b.dot(b) - tolSec):
               a,FA,b,M = cand_s,FA_s,b_s,M_s
               reject_stag=0; max_stag += 1? # no max improve
       # If M currently much worse than best? maybe if perturbed, best still. OK.
       # If no max improvement very long, maybe try sym? done.
    return best_score, best_a
```

Pseudo final code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _next_pow2(x): ...
def _normalize(a, fallback_n=64): ...

def _local_search(a0, until, rng, alpha_init=0.7):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 1:
        return None
    L = _next_pow2(max(2*n-1, 3*n-2))
    Lc = 2*n-1
    # floor
    a = a + (1e-12 / n)
    a = a / a.sum()
    FA = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    def state_from(x, FAx=None):
       if FAx is None: FAx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
       bf = np.fft.irfft(FAx * FAx, L)
       np.maximum(bf, 0.0, out=bf)
       bb = bf[:Lc]
       Mx = float(bb.max()) if bb.size else 0.0
       return FAx, bb, Mx
    _, b, M = state_from(a, FA)
    best_score = 2.0*n*M
    best_a = a.copy()
    ...
```

Final code for propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = min(60.0, max(5.0, 0.05*total))
    final_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.1, total - reserve)
    try:
       a_parent = _normalize(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
       a_parent = None
    if a_parent is None or a_parent.size == 0:
       # fallback random length 512? maybe 1024
       n0 = 1024
       a_parent = _normalize(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n0)))
    # maybe initial small perturb? We'll handle in local.
    res = _local_search(a_parent, final_deadline, rng, alpha_init=0.7)
    if res is None:
       best_a = a_parent
    else:
       best_score, best_a, cur_a = res
    # final validation
    candidates = []
    if best_a is not None: candidates.append((best_a, None))
    if cur_a is not None and cur_a is not best_a: candidates.append((cur_a, None))
    candidates.append((a_parent, None))
    # uniform fallback
    uni = np.ones(a_parent.size if a_parent is not None else 64, dtype=np.float64)
    uni /= uni.sum()
    candidates.append((uni, 2.0*uni.size)) # internal score
    best_out = None
    best_exact = np.inf
    best_int = np.inf
    for arr in candidates:
       try:
          if arr is None: continue
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): continue
          sc = float('inf')
          try:
              sc = float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
          except Exception:
              sc = np.inf
          if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
              best_exact = sc; best_out = arr
          # also internal if no exact? maybe compute expensive? not needed
       except Exception: pass
      if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 1: break
    if best_out is None:
       best_out = uni
    # ensure list finite nonempty
    out = _normalize(best_out)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```

In `_normalize`, if `a.size==0` after ravel, fallback. If `a` is scalar? ravel size1.

```
def _normalize(a, fallback_n=64):
    try:
       a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
       if a.ndim == 0: a = a.reshape(1)
       else: a = a.ravel()
    except Exception:
       a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
       a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
       a = np.ones(a.size if a.size > 0 else fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
       s = float(a.sum())
    a = a + (1e-16 / max(1, a.size))
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    return a
```
If parent has very small sum <1e-12, fallback uniform of same size. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize(a, fallback_n=64):
    """Return a finite, nonnegative, capped, sum-to-one numpy array."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.ndim == 0:
            a = a.reshape(1)
        else:
            a = a.ravel()
    except Exception:
        a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)

    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(max(1, int(a.size)), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())

    # A tiny floor helps avoid irreversible zeros during multiplicative moves.
    a = a + (1e-16 / max(1, int(a.size)))
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    return a


def _local_search(a0, until, rng, alpha_init=0.7):
    """
    Local multiplicative/non-Euclidean search for minimizing
    max_k conv(a,a)_k with sum(a)=1.

    Returns (best_internal_score, best_array, current_array).
    """
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, None, None

    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(max(Lc, 3 * n - 2))

    def state_from(x, FAx=None):
        if FAx is None:
            FAx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
        bf = np.fft.irfft(FAx * FAx, L)
        np.maximum(bf, 0.0, out=bf)
        bb = bf[:Lc]
        Mx = float(bb.max()) if bb.size else 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(Mx):
            # Fallback for pathological numerical states.
            x = np.ones_like(x) / float(x.size)
            FAx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
            bf = np.fft.irfft(FAx * FAx, L)
            np.maximum(bf, 0.0, out=bf)
            bb = bf[:Lc]
            Mx = float(bb.max()) if bb.size else 0.0
        return FAx, bb, Mx

    FA, b, M = state_from(a)
    score0 = 2.0 * n * M
    best_score = score0
    best_a = a.copy()

    alpha = float(alpha_init)
    min_alpha = 0.03
    max_alpha = 1.8

    max_stag = 0          # iterations since last exact-max improvement
    reject_stag = 0       # consecutive rejected moves
    perturb_sigma = 0.035
    tau_frac = 0.20       # softmax temperature fraction for very focused mode
    active_frac = 0.20    # active-set tolerance for near-max lags

    tolM = 1e-16
    tolSec = 1e-16

    rp_buf = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

    def grad_l2(FAx):
        # Gradient (up to positive scale) of sum_k c_k^2.
        # c = a*a, and grad_i ~ sum_k c_k a_{k-i}.
        Gf = np.fft.irfft((FAx * FAx) * np.conj(FAx), L)
        G = Gf[:n]
        np.maximum(G, 0.0, out=G)
        return G

    def grad_p(p, FAx):
        # For arbitrary normalized weights p on convolution lags:
        # G_i = sum_k p_k a_{k-i}.
        rp_buf[:Lc] = p[::-1]
        if Lc < L:
            rp_buf[Lc:] = 0.0
        Fp = np.fft.rfft(rp_buf, L)
        Gf = np.fft.irfft(FAx * Fp, L)
        G = Gf[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        np.maximum(G, 0.0, out=G)
        return G

    def weights_power(bvec, q):
        # p_k proportional to c_k^q, for q > 1.
        bpos = bvec + 1e-300
        lb = np.log(bpos)
        lp = q * lb
        lp -= float(lp.max())
        p = np.exp(lp)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(bvec))] = 1.0
        else:
            p = p / s
        return p

    def weights_active(bvec, Mx, frac):
        p = (bvec >= max(0.0, Mx * (1.0 - frac))).astype(np.float64)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 0:
            return p / s
        p = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(bvec))] = 1.0
        return p

    def weights_softmax(bvec, Mx, tfrac):
        tau = max(1e-30, float(tfrac) * float(Mx))
        z = (bvec - Mx) / tau
        np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
        p = np.exp(z)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(bvec))] = 1.0
        else:
            p = p / s
        return p

    def make_candidate(a_cur, Gv, al, style):
        gm = float(Gv.mean())
        if not math.isfinite(gm) or gm <= 1e-300:
            return a_cur.copy(), False

        x = Gv / gm

        # Larger steps are more stable in log/multiplicative form.
        eff_style = 1 if (style == 1 or al > 0.8) else 0

        if eff_style == 0:
            factor = 1.0 + al * (1.0 - x)
            np.clip(factor, 1e-8, 100.0, out=factor)
        else:
            logx = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-12))
            center = float(logx.mean())
            if not math.isfinite(center):
                return a_cur.copy(), False
            logfactor = -al * (logx - center)
            np.clip(logfactor, -5.0, 5.0, out=logfactor)
            factor = np.exp(logfactor)

        cand = a_cur * factor
        np.maximum(cand, 0.0, out=cand)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return a_cur.copy(), False
        cand /= s
        return cand, True

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < until:
        it += 1

        # Select weights: flatten by L2 first, then progressively focus
        # on the largest convolution coefficients.
        if max_stag < 30:
            G = grad_l2(FA)
            p = b
            sec_old = float(np.dot(p, p))  # sum b^2, since b ~ normalized
            style = 0
        elif max_stag < 100:
            p = weights_power(b, 2.0)
            G = grad_p(p, FA)
            sec_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
            style = 0
        elif max_stag < 250:
            p = weights_power(b, 5.0)
            G = grad_p(p, FA)
            sec_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
            style = 1
        elif max_stag < 550:
            p = weights_active(b, M, active_frac)
            G = grad_p(p, FA)
            sec_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
            style = 1
        else:
            p = weights_softmax(b, M, tau_frac)
            G = grad_p(p, FA)
            sec_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
            style = 1

        cand, ok = make_candidate(a, G, alpha, style)
        if not ok:
            reject_stag += 1
            max_stag += 1
            alpha = max(min_alpha, alpha * 0.55)
            if reject_stag > 80 or alpha <= min_alpha * 1.2:
                ta = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, perturb_sigma, n))
                ta = _normalize(ta)
                FA, b, M = state_from(ta)
                a = ta
                reject_stag = 0
                max_stag = 0
                alpha = 0.7
                tau_frac = 0.2
                active_frac = 0.2
                perturb_sigma = min(0.45, perturb_sigma * 1.3)
                sc = 2.0 * n * M
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
            continue

        FA_new, b_new, M_new = state_from(cand)
        sec_new = float(np.dot(p, b_new))

        improved_max = M_new < M - tolM
        accept = False
        if improved_max:
            accept = True
        elif M_new <= M + tolM and sec_new < sec_old - tolSec:
            accept = True

        if accept:
            a = cand
            FA = FA_new
            b = b_new
            M = M_new
            reject_stag = 0

            if improved_max:
                sc = 2.0 * n * M
                if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                max_stag = 0
                alpha = min(max_alpha, alpha * 1.12)
                # After a real max improvement, keep temperatures moderate.
                tau_frac = min(0.25, tau_frac * 1.10)
                active_frac = min(0.25, active_frac * 1.10)
            else:
                max_stag += 1
                alpha = min(max_alpha * 0.85, alpha * 1.03)
        else:
            reject_stag += 1
            max_stag += 1
            alpha = max(min_alpha, alpha * 0.62)

            if max_stag >= 250:
                active_frac = max(0.02, active_frac * 0.75)
                tau_frac = max(0.01, tau_frac * 0.85)

            if reject_stag > 90:
                tau_frac = max(0.01, tau_frac * 0.80)

            if reject_stag > 160 or (reject_stag > 60 and alpha <= min_alpha * 1.05):
                # Multiplicative regrafting.
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, perturb_sigma, n)
                ta = a * np.exp(noise)
                ta = _normalize(ta)
                FA, b, M = state_from(ta)
                a = ta
                reject_stag = 0
                max_stag = 0
                alpha = 0.75
                tau_frac = 0.20
                active_frac = 0.20
                perturb_sigma = min(0.50, perturb_sigma * 1.35)
                sc = 2.0 * n * M
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                continue

        # Periodic symmetrization probe: reversal leaves max unchanged,
        # and averaging with the reversal can reduce asymmetric peaks.
        if it % 180 == 0 and (max_stag > 30 or reject_stag > 15):
            cand_s = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
            cand_s = _normalize(cand_s)
            FA_s, b_s, M_s = state_from(cand_s)
            l2_old = float(np.dot(b, b))
            l2_s = float(np.dot(b_s, b_s))

            if M_s < M - tolM:
                a = cand_s
                FA = FA_s
                b = b_s
                M = M_s
                reject_stag = 0
                max_stag = 0
                sc = 2.0 * n * M
                if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
            elif M_s <= M + tolM and l2_s < l2_old - tolSec:
                a = cand_s
                FA = FA_s
                b = b_s
                M = M_s
                reject_stag = 0
                max_stag += 1

    return best_score, best_a, a.copy()


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = min(30.0, max(6.0, 0.02 * total))
    final_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.5, total - reserve)

    # Parent construction, if provided.
    try:
        a_parent = _normalize(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        a_parent = None

    if a_parent is None or a_parent.size == 0:
        # Compact smooth fallback if the parent is unavailable.
        n0 = 2048
        x = np.arange(n0, dtype=np.float64)
        base = np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64)
        for m, amp in ((1, 0.45), (2, 0.28), (3, 0.18), (5, 0.12)):
            base += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x / max(1, n0) + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a_parent = _normalize(np.maximum(base, 0.05))

    start = time.monotonic()
    main_until = start + 0.78 * max(0.0, final_deadline - start)

    res = _local_search(a_parent, main_until, rng, alpha_init=0.70)
    best_score, best_a, cur_a = res if res is not None else (np.inf, a_parent, a_parent)

    # If enough time remains, do one controlled randomized continuation.
    rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_a is not None and best_a.size > 0:
        n_best = int(best_a.size)
        noisy = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, n_best))
        rnd = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45, n_best))
        rnd = rnd / max(1e-300, float(rnd.sum()))
        mix = 0.12
        seed2 = (1.0 - mix) * noisy + mix * rnd
        seed2 = _normalize(seed2)

        until2 = final_deadline - max(3.0, 0.02 * total)
        if until2 > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
            res2 = _local_search(seed2, until2, rng, alpha_init=0.75)
            if res2 is not None:
                s2, b2, c2 = res2
                if math.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                    best_score = s2
                    best_a = b2
                    cur_a = c2
                elif math.isfinite(s2) and (cur_a is None or not math.isfinite(best_score)):
                    best_score = s2
                    best_a = b2
                    cur_a = c2

    # Final safety/filtering.
    candidates = []
    if best_a is not None and np.isfinite(np.asarray(best_a)).all():
        candidates.append(best_a)
    if cur_a is not None and np.isfinite(np.asarray(cur_a)).all():
        candidates.append(cur_a)
    if a_parent is not None and np.isfinite(np.asarray(a_parent)).all():
        candidates.append(a_parent)

    n_final = a_parent.size if a_parent is not None and a_parent.size > 0 else 64
    uniform = np.ones(int(n_final), dtype=np.float64)
    uniform = _normalize(uniform)
    candidates.append(uniform)

    chosen = None
    best_exact = np.inf

    for arr in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 1.0:
            break
        try:
            arr = _normalize(arr)
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc
                chosen = arr
        except Exception:
            pass

    if chosen is None:
        # If exact evaluation is somehow unavailable, fall back internally.
        if best_a is not None and math.isfinite(best_score):
            chosen = best_a
        elif a_parent is not None:
            chosen = a_parent
        else:
            chosen = uniform

    out = _normalize(chosen)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
